Title: Historia Reformationis Polonicae, in qua tum Reformatorum, tum Antitrinitariorum origo et progressus in Polonia et finitimis Provinciis narrantur
Creator: Stanislaw Lubieniecki (Stanislaus Lubieniecius), Polish Brother, author
Publisher: Freistadii (the title page; commonly Amsterdam): apud Johannem Aconium, 1685 (host record: Johannes Aconius, Henricus Wetstein, Benedykt Wiszowaty)
Publication Date: 1685
Rights: Public Domain (published 1685; public domain by date; host rights field "Domena publiczna (public domain)")
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item jbc.bj.uj.edu.pl.DIGSTDR000348, file DIGSTDR000348_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for anti-trinitarian-currents-servetus-polish-brethr), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: "Domena publiczna (public domain)" (contributor Biblioteka Jagiellonska).
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (0 occurrences, none present). Rights basis restated: a 1685 imprint, read off the scanned title page, is public domain by date, and the host marks it public domain.
Content note: The whole volume: the publisher's note to the reader (which says the author was taken by early death before finishing), the Index capitum (file line 617), and the history beginning at Liber 1 (line 758): the Polish Reformation and the rise of the Antitrinitarians in Poland, by a Polish Brother who wrote from the movement's own side. The number of books was not counted. OCR sampled at five points: Latin readable but with long-s read as f, scattered Cyrillic-looking glyphs and some column mix-up (word-like share about 0.85); quote only after checking the page image. A second scan of the same edition (bub_gb_mSK5n_GsGHwC, Rome copy) is more heavily garbled and was not vendored. No public-domain English translation exists (Lubieniecki's History of the Polish Reformation appeared in English in 1995, in copyright).
